There are two steps to make bulk payments

## Step 1: Initiate the Payment

Use the [CBX Nodal Bulk Translation API
](https: //hdfc-group.readme.io/spliceforms/v3.0/reference/post_api-cbx-sandbox-cbx-nodal-bulkpayment) to submit the request

### Request

<Table align={
    [
        "left",
        "left"
    ]
}>
  <thead>
    <tr>
      <th>
        Request Fields
      </th>

      <th>
        Description
      </th>
    </tr>
  </thead>

  <tbody>
    <tr>
      <td>
        `clientcode`
      </td>

      <td>
        Client Code
      </td>
    </tr>

    <tr>
      <td>
        `groupid`
      </td>

      <td>
        Domain ID
      </td>
    </tr>

    <tr>
      <td>
        `iduser`
      </td>

      <td>
        Maker ID
      </td>
    </tr>

    <tr>
      <td>
        `nooftran`
      </td>

      <td>
        Number of transcations
      </td>
    </tr>

    <tr>
      <td>
        Array of `trans`
      </td>

      <td>
        Refer to [specification details
](https: //hdfc-group.readme.io/spliceforms/v3.0/reference/specification-details#for-bulk-transaction-api) for more information.  

        `code` (Beneficiary code) in the request is mandatory for making a payment to a registered beneficiary, otherwise the param is optional
      </td>
    </tr>
  </tbody>
</Table>



> 📘 Note
>
> Depending on your <Glossary>ENET</Glossary> configuration, payment to non-registered beneficiaries *may or may not* be permitted - please speak with your implementation manager

### Response

| Response Fields | Description                                                                                |
| :-------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `txtstatus`     | Payment request status                                                                     |
| `batchnum`      | Batch number. This number is used in the unquiry API to unquire the status of the payments |

## Step 2: Inquire the Status

Use the [CBX Nodal Bulk Inquiry API
](https: //hdfc-group.readme.io/spliceforms/v3.0/reference/post_api-cbx-sandbox-cbx-nodal-bulkpaymentinq) to get the status of the outward remittance transaction. This API will help in inquiring and getting the status of the transaction with the help of a unique reference number of the generated transaction through Bulk Payment API Call.

> 📘 Note
>
> This API may be called immediately, and periodically after 30s intervals, up to a max of 15 mins

### Request

| Request Fields | Description                                                               |
| :------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `batchnum`     | The CBX API batch number returned in the bulk payment initiation API call |
| `reqdexctndt`  | Transaction date in DD/MM/YYYY format                                     |
| `gcif`         | Domain ID                                                                 |
| `iduser`       | Maker ID                                                                  |

```shell Example (Request)
curl --request POST \
     --url https: //api-uat.hdfcbank.com/API/cbx-nodal-bulkPaymentInq \
     --header 'accept: application/json' \
     --header 'apikey: aHNDiZ5fj52HXscBQMBEJwdG3BBNjBNNaeCyKt5ff4DL6RmO' \
     --header 'authorization: Bearer token' \
     --header 'content-type: application/json' \
     --data '
{
    "gcif": "NODALT",
    "iduser": "1MAKER",
    "batchnum": "NODALT4903API202212231437490030",
    "reqdexctndt": "23/12/2022"
}
'
```

### Response

| Response Fields                       | Description                                                   |
| :------------------------------------ | :------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `trans` as list of Transaction object | `codstatus` describe the status of the submitted transaction. |

